<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">It was narrated from &#x27;Abdullah bin &#x27;Abdur-Rahman bin Abu Sa&#x27;sa&#x27;ah that:His father who was under the care of Abu Sa&#x27;eed said: &quot;Abu Sa&#x27;eed said to me: &#x27;If you are in the desert, raise your voice when you say the Adhan, for I heard the Messenger of Allah say: &#x27;No jinn, human, tree or rock will hear it, but it will bear witness for you</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Ibn Majah · 723]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/ibnmajah/723" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Ibn Majah</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अब्दुर-रहमान बिन अबू सा&#x27;सा&#x27;यह (जो अबू सईद खुदरी (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) के ज़ेर-इ-परवरिश थे) कहते हैं कि मुझसे अब सईद (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हु) ने कहा: जब तुम सेहरा में हो तोह अज़ान में अपनी आवाज़ बुलंद करो अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) को फरमाते हुए सुना है: अज़ान को जिन्नात वो क़यामत के दिन उसके लिए गवाही देंगे.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan Ibn Majah · 723]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/ibnmajah/723" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Ibn Majah</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">عبدالرحمٰن بن ابوصعصعہ (جو ابو سعید خدری رضی اللہ عنہ کے زیر پرورش تھے) کہتے ہیں کہ مجھ سے ابوسعید رضی اللہ عنہ نے کہا: جب تم صحراء میں ہو تو اذان میں اپنی آواز بلند کرو، اس لیے کہ میں نے رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کو فرماتے ہوئے سنا ہے: اذان کو جنات، انسان، درخت اور پتھر جو بھی سنیں گے وہ قیامت کے دن اس کے لیے گواہی دیں گے ۱؎۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Ibn Majah · 723]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/ibnmajah/723" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Ibn Majah</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abdur-Rahman bin Abu Sa&#x27;sa&#x27;ah (jo Abu Saeed Khudri (Radi Allahu Anhu) ke zer-e-parwarish the) kehte hain ki mujhse Abu Saeed (Radi Allahu Anhu) ne kaha: Jab tum sehra mein ho toh azan mein apni aawaz buland karo, isliye ki maine Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ko farmate hue suna hai: Azan ko jinnaat, insaan, darakht aur patthar jo bhi sunenge wo Qayamat ke din uske liye gawahi denge.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Ibn Majah · 723]</p>

## Narration 724

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-ibnmajah%3A724)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 3</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا أَبُو بَكْرِ بْنُ أَبِي شَيْبَةَ، حَدَّثَنَا شَبَابَةُ، حَدَّثَنَا شُعْبَةُ، عَنْ مُوسَى بْنِ أَبِي عُثْمَانَ، عَنْ أَبِي يَحْيَى، عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ، قَالَ سَمِعْتُ مِنْ، فِي رَسُولِ اللَّهِ ـ صلى الله عليه وسلم ـ يَقُولُ ‏ &quot;‏ الْمُؤَذِّنُ يُغْفَرُ لَهُ مَدَّ صَوْتِهِ وَيَسْتَغْفِرُ لَهُ كُلُّ رَطْبٍ وَيَابِسٍ وَشَاهِدُ الصَّلاَةِ يُكْتَبُ لَهُ خَمْسٌ وَعِشْرُونَ حَسَنَةً وَيُكَفَّرُ عَنْهُ مَا بَيْنَهُمَا ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Ibn Majah · 724]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/ibnmajah/724" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Ibn Majah</a></p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>Arabic · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sunan Ibn Majah · 724</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan Ibn Majah   724 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">It was narrated that Abu Hurairah said:&quot;I heard the Messenger of Allah himself say: &#x27;The Mu&#x27;adh-dhin&#x27;s sins will be forgiven as far as his voice reaches, and every wet and dry thing will pray for forgiveness for him. For the one who attends the prayer, twenty-five Hasanat (good deeds) will be recorded, and it is will be expiation (for sins committed) between them (the two prayers)</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Ibn Majah · 724]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/ibnmajah/724" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Ibn Majah</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">
<summary>English · references (1)</summary>

<p><strong>Sunan Ibn Majah · 724</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

<p dir="auto">[ Sunan Ibn Majah   724 ]</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Sources credited by this publisher</summary>

<p><a href="https://github.com/fawazahmed0/hadith-api">Fawaz Ahmed Hadith API</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

<p><a href="https://sunnah.com/">Sunnah.com</a></p>

<p dir="auto">site level attribution not record verified</p>

</details>

</section>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">अबू हुरैरह (रदियल्लाहु अन्हु) कहते हैं कि मैंने रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) को फ़रमाते हुए सुना: मुअज़्ज़िन की आवाज़ जहाँ तक पहुँचती है उसकी मग़फ़िरत कर दी जाती है, और हर ख़ुश्क-ओ-तर उसके लिए मग़फ़िरत तलब करता है, और अज़ान सुन कर नमाज़ में हाज़िर होने वाले के लिए पचीस (25) नेकियाँ लिखी जाती हैं, और दो नमाज़ों के दरमियान के गुनाह बख़्श दिए जाते हैं।</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan Ibn Majah · 724]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/ibnmajah/724" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Ibn Majah</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details class="reading-references">

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">ابوہریرہ رضی اللہ عنہ کہتے ہیں کہ میں نے رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم کو فرماتے ہوئے سنا: مؤذن کی آواز جہاں تک پہنچتی ہے اس کی مغفرت کر دی جاتی ہے، اور ہر خشک و تر اس کے لیے مغفرت طلب کرتا ہے، اور اذان سن کر نماز میں حاضر ہونے والے کے لیے پچیس ( ۲۵ ) نیکیاں لکھی جاتی ہیں، اور دو نمازوں کے درمیان کے گناہ بخش دئیے جاتے ہیں ۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Ibn Majah · 724]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/ibnmajah/724" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Ibn Majah</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur-Latn">
<h3>Roman Urdu</h3>

<p dir="ltr">Abu Hurairah (Radi Allahu Anhu) kehte hain ki maine Rasool Allah (Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam) ko farmate hue suna: Muazzin ki aawaz jahan tak pahunchti hai uski maghfirat kar di jati hai, aur har khushk-o-tar uske liye maghfirat talab karta hai, aur azan sun kar namaz mein hazir hone wale ke liye pachis (25) nekiyan likhi jati hain, aur do namazon ke darmiyan ke gunah bakhsh diye jate hain.</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Ibn Majah · 724]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/ibnmajah/724" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Ibn Majah</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

## Narration 725

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-ibnmajah%3A725)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 3</strong></p>

<p><strong>Hadith</strong> · A narration preserved from the source.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="rtl">حَدَّثَنَا مُحَمَّدُ بْنُ بَشَّارٍ، وَإِسْحَاقُ بْنُ مَنْصُورٍ، قَالاَ حَدَّثَنَا أَبُو عَامِرٍ، حَدَّثَنَا سُفْيَانُ، عَنْ طَلْحَةَ بْنِ يَحْيَى، عَنْ عِيسَى بْنِ طَلْحَةَ، قَالَ سَمِعْتُ مُعَاوِيَةَ بْنَ أَبِي سُفْيَانَ، قَالَ قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ـ صلى الله عليه وسلم ـ ‏ &quot;‏ الْمُؤَذِّنُونَ أَطْوَلُ النَّاسِ أَعْنَاقًا يَوْمَ الْقِيَامَةِ ‏&quot;‏ ‏.‏</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Ibn Majah · 725]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/ibnmajah/725" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Ibn Majah</a></p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="ltr">It was narrated that &#x27;Esa bin Talhah said:&quot;I heard Mu&#x27;awiyah bin Abu Sufyan say that Messenger of Allah said: &quot;The Mu&#x27;adh-dhin will have the longest necks of all people on the Day of Resurrection</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Sunan Ibn Majah · 725]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/ibnmajah/725" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Sunan Ibn Majah</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="hi">
<h3>Hindi</h3>

<p dir="auto">मुआवियाह बिन आबि सुफ़यान (रज़ियल्लाहु अन्हुमा) कहते हैं कि रसूल अल्लाह (सल्लल्लाहु अलैहि वसल्लम) ने फ़रमाया: मुअज़्ज़िनों की गर्दनें क़यामत के दिन सब से ज़्यादा लम्बी होगी.</p>

<p dir="auto">[Sunan Ibn Majah · 725]</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ur">
<h3>Urdu</h3>

<p dir="rtl">معاویہ بن ابی سفیان رضی اللہ عنہما کہتے ہیں کہ رسول اللہ صلی اللہ علیہ وسلم نے فرمایا: مؤذنوں کی گردنیں قیامت کے دن سب سے زیادہ لمبی ہوں گی ۱؎۔</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Sunan Ibn Majah · 725]</p>
